2636681 1833615 Thu Oct  8 17:15:33 2026 bash            /bin/bash -lc set -o pipefail V095_DIR='本地实验/R-W4-4/V095/partial-ranking' V095_PARENT_EXE="$V095_DIR/stage/build-v095/clx_ref_parent_probe" V095_CANDIDATE_EXE="$V095_DIR/stage/build-v095/clx_ref_candidate_probe" {   printf 'ROUTE=MODE-DISPATCH-CUTOFF-X\nREVISION=V095\nSTAGE=RUNTIME_ENV_AND_LINK_CHECK\nSTART_UTC=%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)"   source /usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/set_env.sh   V095_ENV_RC=$?
